\displaystyle I_{j}=\begin{cases}[a_{j},a_{j+1})&\quad\mbox{if }0\leq j<2N,\\
[a_{2N},a_{2N+1}]&\quad\mbox{if }j=2N.\end{cases}

Using
proposition

F-1
, we have

\displaystyle\inf_{F\in\pazocal{F}_{\alpha}(w,[q_{l},q_{h}])}R(\Psi,F)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\inf_{x\in[\underline{r}_{\alpha}(w,q_{l}),w)}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|x,(w,q_{l})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{l}))d\Psi(u),

\displaystyle\inf_{x\in[w,\overline{r}_{\alpha}(w,q_{h})]}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|x,(w,q_{h})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{h})))d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\inf_{x\in[a_{i},a_{i+1})}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|x,(w,q_{l})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{l}))d\Psi(u),

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\inf_{x\in[a_{i},a_{i+1}]}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|x,(w,q_{h})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{h})))d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}.

Following the same reasoning as in the proof of
Theorem

4
, we may also obtain an upper bound on
\inf_{F\in\pazocal{F}_{\alpha}(w,[q_{l},q_{h}])}R(\Psi,F)
. Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\inf_{F\in\pazocal{F}_{\alpha}(w,[q_{l},q_{h}])}R(\Psi,F)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))d\Psi(u),

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\int_{0}^{\infty}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\sum_{j=0}^{2N}\int_{I_{j}}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))d\Psi(u),

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\sum_{j=0}^{2N}\int_{I_{j}}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}.

For
x\in[\underline{r}_{\alpha}(w,q_{l}),w)
, the revenue function
u\mapsto u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(\cdot|x,(w,q_{l}))
is increasing in
u
on
[0,x)
and decreasing on
(x,w)
. In addition, note that, for
x\in[w,\overline{r}_{\alpha}(w,q_{h})]
, the revenue function
u\mapsto u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{h}))
is non-decreasing on
[0,x]
and
u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|x,(w,q_{h}))=0
for
u>x
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\inf_{F\in\pazocal{F}_{\alpha}(w,[q_{l},q_{h}])}R(\Psi,F)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\sum_{j=0}^{2N}\int_{I_{j}}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))d\Psi(u),

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\sum_{j=0}^{2N}\int_{I_{j}}u\overline{F}_{\alpha}(u|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\Biggl[\sum_{j=0}^{i}a_{j+1}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j+1}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))\int_{I_{j}}d\Psi(u)+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=i+1}^{2N}a_{j}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))\int_{I_{j}}d\Psi(u)\Biggl],

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\sum_{j=0}^{2N}a_{j+1}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j+1}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))\int_{I_{j}}d\Psi(u)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min\Biggl\{\min_{i=1,\cdots,N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\Biggl[\sum_{j=1}^{i+1}a_{j}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))p_{j}+

\displaystyle\sum_{j=i+2}^{2N+1}a_{j-1}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j-1}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))p_{j}\Biggl],

\displaystyle\min_{i=N+1,\cdots,2N}\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\sum_{j=1}^{2N+1}a_{j}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))p_{j}\Biggr\}.

The problem of maximizing over mechanisms in
\pazocal{P}
is clearly upper bounded by the problem of maximizing the RHS above over
p_{1},...,p_{2N+1}
. The epigraph formulation of the latter problem can be written as

\displaystyle\overline{\pazocal{L}_{U}}_{\alpha,q_{l},q_{h},\mathbb{A}}\>\>=\>\>\max_{\mathbf{p},c}

\displaystyle~~c

(G-1)

\displaystyle s.t.~~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{l})))}\Biggl[\sum_{j=1}^{i+1}a_{j}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))p_{j}+\sum_{j=i+2}^{2N+1}a_{j-1}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j-1}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{l}))p_{j}\Biggl]\geq c

\displaystyle i=1,...N,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mbox{opt}(F_{\alpha}(\cdot|a_{i},(w,q_{h})))}\sum_{j=1}^{2N+1}a_{j}\overline{F}_{\alpha}(a_{j}|a_{i+1}^{-},(w,q_{h}))p_{j}\geq c\quad i=N+1,...2N,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{2N+1}p_{j}\leq 1,\quad p_{i}\geq 0\quad i=1,...2N+1.

Therefore we obtain that:

\displaystyle\pazocal{R}(\pazocal{P},\pazocal{F}_{\alpha}(w,[q_{l},q_{h}]))\leq\overline{\pazocal{L}_{U}}_{\alpha,q_{l},q_{h},\mathbb{A}}.

Implementation parameters:

For all reported values in the main text, we use the following sequence in the Linear Programs

\displaystyle a_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\underline{r}_{\alpha}(1,q_{l})+\frac{i}{N}\left(1-\eta-\underline{r}_{\alpha}(1,q_{l})\right)&\quad\mbox{if }0\leq i\leq N.\\
w+\frac{i-N-1}{N}\left(\min(b,\overline{r}_{\alpha}(1,q_{h}))-1\right)&\quad\mbox{if }N+1\leq i\leq 2N+1,\\
\end{cases}

with
N=2500,\eta=10^{-5},b=250
.
